## Character processes and local subjects

Local subject keys are retrieval identities, not enrolled global character IDs. Aliases preserve reader and participant knowledge separately.

| Local subject ID | Verified identity/aliases at V01 | Initial character process and boundary | Evidence / claims |
|---|---|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-MONICA | モニカ・エヴァレット; 〈沈黙の魔女〉; cover モニカ・ノートン, later ノートン会計 | At 17, two years a sage. Anticipated judgment constricts speech and bodily action; familiar nonhuman contact and absorbed technical interest permit different response ranges. She values numbers aesthetically and through paternal memory, uses them to retreat from pain, and increasingly values social gifts. New effort is prompted by a wish to thank and protect, not a complete confidence gain. Technical mastery coexists with poor flying/balance, limited court knowledge, inadequate nourishment and difficult narrative reporting. Reserve admission and talent-as-curse are attributed self-accounts with different support. | E002–004, E008–012, E015, E017–019, E021–023; C001–003, C009–010 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-LOUIS | ルイス・ミラー; 〈結界の魔術師〉; 同期殿 is his peer address to Monica | Professionally powerful, publicly polished and privately abrasive. He recruits through force and inflated fear, recognizes exceptional talent, supplies infrastructure and backup, and enjoys challenging strong people. Losing to Monica makes her self-deprecation injure his own standing. His stated confidence-building aim coexists with that interest. With Rosalie he seeks affection and accepts medical priority; impending fatherhood reorganizes his sense of urgent news. His inference about royal surveillance is not royal testimony. | E002–004, E005, E018, E022–023; C006 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-NERO | ネロ; familiar in black-cat and human forms; fictitious バーソロミュー・アレクサンダー | Reads fiction to acquire human roles, vocabulary and standards; often overconfident about them. Practical affection includes paw contact, scouting and rescue. He can understand the effect of pain on Monica yet ignore her refusal during practice. Wants praise and regards her as his favorite, with a private threat against Felix. Human laws do not automatically bind his self-understanding. Nonhuman species is not identified; he is sensitive to magic and vulnerable to cold. | E002–003, E007–010, E012, E015, E019–020 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-ISABELLE | イザベル・ノートン; daughter of ケルベック伯爵アズール・ノートン | Territorial duty, experienced loss and gratitude motivate cooperation. Fiction supplies a desired villainess role, which she works at as a craft; her public harshness can still frighten Monica. She wants intimacy (room-sharing, tea, matching hair) but provides mission-compatible distance and accepts Agatha's limits. Her ideal of voluntary brave/modest rescue is explicitly incomplete. | E001, E004–007, E016 |

Service includes initiative and correction of her mistress, not only obedience. No broad interior account beyond these scenes. | E001, E016 |

Worry, partial acknowledgment of provoking Caroline and unchanged care after the appointment challenge Monica's expectation of rejection. Praise from Felix also fuels vanity and craft ambition. Overtraining produces dangerous absorption, while the brooch means belonging. Loyalty to Felix activates rage and punitive threats; ordinary injury and disputed blame activate concern and witness checking. He acknowledges Monica's accurate work before accepting her manner, then offers role-specific recognition and accompaniment. Recognition of her as rescuer remains suspected, not confirmed. | E006–007, E012–013, E015–017, E019–020, E022; C004, C010 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-FELIX | フェリクス・アーク・リディル; second prince, 75th council president; final scenes retrospectively connect opening observer | Gentle presentation can deliver threat, concealment or practical help. Grandfather's control and his own need to appear competent constrain him; his unexplained “remaining life” before succession qualifies carefree-boredom accounts. Has undisclosed magical literacy and a contracted spirit. Reveres Lady Everett without recognizing her in Monica Norton. | E001, E006, E009, E011, E013–017, E020, E022–023; C005, C008 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-SELMA | セルマ・カーシュ; health representative, Aaron's fiancée | Titles of friend and fiancée prevent a sense of having nothing, even when she knows the relationships are unequal. Another person's assurance that only she can save Aaron intensifies self-sacrifice; mental interference later complicates attribution of her choices. Care in the sickroom coexists with endangering Monica. Total confession overstates what is established and tries to redirect guilt. Neither pure devotion nor pure manipulation by another exhausts the presented evidence. | E009, E012, E014, E021, E023; C007 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-S-AARON | アーロン・オブライエン; ステイル伯爵's son, removed treasurer | Patronage purchased by his family lowers his spending money; he repeatedly embezzles for pleasure with assistance. She treats unresponsiveness as insolence, missing its process; no private motive of jealousy is established. By exit she has not accepted Monica according to Felix. His friendly approach corrects a universal-hostility expectation. Reference describes suggestibility and oversized uniform; no broader operational personality inferred from it. | E006, E015, E017, E022; reference embed0020 |

## Directional relationships

Each record holds two arrows without making their motivations equal.

| SW-CG2-20261019-R-001 Monica → Louis / Louis → Monica | She fears punishment, credits his competence, resists some demands and congratulates the family from remembered care. He controls the assignment and information, recognizes strength she denies, has backup and reduces oral-report demands, while using humiliation and force. His low estimate of execution risk has not been given to her. | E003–005, E018, E022–023; C006 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-002 Monica → Nero / Nero → Monica | She trusts familiar cat contact and can speak thanks, quarrel, correct names or ignore absurd claims; human form can still frighten her. He supplies companionship, grounding and practical help, but seeks praise, ignores some requests and claims protective possession. Neither is uniformly subordinate despite familiar/master language. | E002–003, E007–010, E012, E015, E018–020 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-003 Isabelle → Monica / Monica → Isabelle | Isabelle admires a savior through a mistaken motive model, offers concrete shelter/social cover and wants intimacy. Monica fears the performance, admires her social skill and worries about her family's reputation. Accepting help need not mean sharing the role fantasy or equal intimacy. | E001, E004–007, E016 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-004 Lana → Monica / Monica → Lana | Lana initiates and corrects sharply, then teaches and worries; praise and being asked matter to her. Monica learns to thank, ask and attempt while still failing, values the ribbon/hair privately, and finds continued friendship despite office a meaningful counterexample. No confirmation that every avoidance of crowds was deliberate accommodation. | E008, E010, E012, E016, E018, E022; C003 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-005 Cyril → Monica / Monica → Cyril | He separates verified competence from disliked behavior, performs fair inquiry and later escorts a recognized treasurer. His intimidation persists alongside care. | E012, E015–017, E019–020, E022; C004 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-006 Monica → Felix / Felix → Monica | Secret duty, gratitude, fear and numerical interest shape her behavior; no reciprocal romantic intention established. He provides help/food/office while imposing touch, testing and authority; has not discovered her sage identity and treats hidden purpose as a game. His separate admiration for Lady Everett is intense but misrouted by identity. | E009, E011, E014–015, E017, E020, E022–023; C005 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-007 Cyril → Felix / Felix → Cyril | Cyril's loyalty permits protection and uncontrolled rage; he obeys a stop and disagrees through appeals to duty. Felix trusts his fairness/work and anticipates his worry, conceals operations/Will and teases him, but also carries him and avoids blaming his collapse. Not equal information or power. | E006, E011, E015, E017–018, E020, E022–023 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-R-008 Elliott → Monica / Monica → Elliott | His explicit class disgust and security suspicion combine; later no acceptance is shown. | SW-CG2-20261019-R-009 Selma → Aaron / Aaron → Selma | She maintains a source of identity despite knowing she is unloved; will claim all guilt. ## Knowledge and chronology

SW-CG2-20261019-K-001 establishes three distinct orders: the prologue rescue occurs three months before the late-summer/early-autumn commission; selection combat is recalled from two years earlier; the main academy action culminates in one night of rescue/capture, followed by a week of aftermath. The supplemental story contains the report written after capture and Louis/Felix views at the one-week aftermath. Its late disclosure does not move the remembered events later in diegetic time. Exact calendar dates are not supplied.

| Knowledge record | Earlier presentation / audience | Eligible exit state and still-separated knowledge | Evidence |
|---|---|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-002 Identity and esteem | Annie admires the witch to Monica; Isabelle admires saved territory without knowing motive. Opening man unnamed. | Reader knows Everett=Norton; Isabelle, Louis, Lin, Nero know her mission identity. School at large does not. Cyril has clues; Felix has suspicion of hidden purpose, not recognition. Final extra retrospectively connects his admiration to the opening. | E001–005, E015, E020, E022–023 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-003 Selection | Monica says reserve/pity admission after interview collapse, with no source for ranking. | Louis recalls complete combat defeat by her and no selector's declared ranking. Her inferiority conclusion is unwarranted; actual formal order remains unknown. This is REVEALED_NOT_NEW ability, not later acquired strength. | E003, E023; C001 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-004 Royal brooch | Louis infers attack and withheld event from destruction; says source hidden via father's gift. | Felix recognized tracking, knows Louis behind it, and ordered Will to break it. Louis's intended secrecy failed. King’s purpose still not confirmed; readers possess both views, Monica has not been shown the final private admission. | E004, E023 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-005 Night excursion | Monica observes proportions; Felix and Elliott construe/accept baiting. | Extra private scene says recreation, not baiting. Elliott and Monica were not shown this correction. Daytime garden baiting was real. Do not conflate the two outings. | E009, E011, E015 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-006 Fraud awareness | Public Felix says he did not immediately detect Aaron; suspects collaborator after attacks. | Private Felix says he let Aaron's fraud accumulate; final extra says he also knew Thornley's involvement. This corrects reader access, not earlier transparency toward council/Monica. | E006, E011, E015, E023 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-007 Two attacks and confession | Similar mechanisms invite same-culprit explanation; Selma confesses to all crimes. | Pot evidence directly identifies her; Thornley is fraud collaborator and uses mental interference on Aaron/Selma/Cyril. Report attributes Selma's escalation to him. Sign confession is less independently corroborated than pot evidence; do not make her global confession proof of embezzlement. | E011–014, E021, E023; C007 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-008 Rescue | Cyril sees a small, silent powerful figure, attacks it and is protected. | Monica/Nero/Lin know the repair. Felix meets human-form Nero, detects nonhuman nature; no identified familiar link. Cyril's callus/small-hand comparison and overheard relief provide suspicion only. | E019–020, E022 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-009 Official closure | Selma/Aaron leave under illness cover; Thornley initially disappears. | Newspaper reports his arrest by Louis; no secret Monica role revealed. Louis arranges handling/leak, treats permanent license loss and exile as appropriate prospective disposition; final sentence not certified. | E014, E021–023 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-K-010 Hidden future | Felix calls succession his life's boundary; Will knows a wish. Louis has a planted disciple. | Explanation/identity withheld. No future source imported. No genre-based identity resolution. | E015, E023 |

## Institutions, mathematics and magic

| Record ID | Rule, demonstration or report and practical consequence | Limits / route |
|---|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-001 | Human magic ordinarily needs chanted formulae; shortened chants still take seconds. Monica can omit chants for roughly 80% of extant spells, not all. Staff length marks rank; seven sages alone may have body-length staffs. Sages hold magic-count rank, including rare female title holders. | Narrator presentation plus examples; E001, E003–005. Spirits' unchanted magic is species capacity, not evidence against Monica's human exception. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-002 | Flight requires control, balance and physical coordination and consumes human reserves rapidly; Monica can leap somewhat but cannot fly competently. Enhanced sight needs an unobstructed path. Magic consumes energy and does not cancel nutrition/sleep needs. | Demonstrated/explicit constraints E009, E018, E020, E022; avoid omnipotence claims from combat. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-003 | Excess absorption can poison a human who fails to stop at capacity. Cyril discharges cold/ice and uses an aid. His unprotected brooch, damaged by force, reverses disposal into return, worsening the crisis. Monica repairs, adds automatic control and double protection. | Mixed narrator exposition, Lin flow verification and Monica analysis E017–019. Made water contains power and can poison in quantity; explains well use despite water magic (E010). |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-004 | Mental interference is restricted/quasi-forbidden, with license consequences outside allowed grave-crime/emergency contexts and permission. It can block memory, induce instability or cause permanent devastation. Study itself is allowed. | E007, E019–021, E023. Monica's sage-exception justification for her own use remains uncertain; no full statutory text or independent ruling. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-005 | Magical devices route power through inscribed formulae and normally need protection. Effects may combine defense, tracking and messages; useful tools can also monitor. Cost and access are class-limited. | E004, E019, E023. Louis's complex protected device and Darwin's simple unprotected one differ; do not infer all devices fail identically. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-006 | Academy education, council positions and patronage are routes to court advancement and possible future royal service. Council president appoints members. Money buys admission and recommendations, while formal performance still matters. Rank order duke/marquis/count/viscount/baron is taught; title may differ from surname. | E004–006, E008, E014–017, E023. Clockford's recommendation causes Aaron's appointment and his hire places Thornley; corruption is institutional opportunity, not evidence everyone is corrupt. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-007 | Room privileges depend on grades/donations; year-separated/shared dorm rules constrain cover. Gloves, attendants, fashion, dining, room applications, locked archives and gatekeeping shape what can be done and known. A known wall crack defeats the ideal of perfect enclosure. | E005–009, E012, E014–017, E020. Ordinary social practices generate detective evidence and cover possibilities. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-008 | Royal factions: first Lionel (27), second Felix (18), third Albert (14), differing mothers; succession unsettled. Louis prominent in first-prince camp, Clockford Felix's maternal grandfather and academy power. King assigns covert guard; Louis interprets it as monitoring. | E004, E014, E023. Caroline's confidence Felix must win is viewpoint-limited political expectation, not determined outcome. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-009 | Minerva specializes in magic, temple institute in law, Serendia in wider elite education. Former noble monopoly on magic education has loosened but wealth/talent gates persist. Noble autumn and commoner spring school starts reflect different labor/social calendars. | E002, E006, E008. Such historical exposition is storyworld presentation, not external social history. |

## Form, themes and evaluation

| Record ID | Proposition and qualification | Evidence / claims |
|---|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-T-001 | Silence functions as speed, concealment, fear-constricted speech, absorption, refusal or withheld knowledge according to scene. Beautiful fluent courtesy can carry coercion. No single silence/personality equivalence. | E001–004, E008–011, E015–017, E019–023; C008 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-T-002 | Numbers are loved forms, paternal relation, affective retreat and investigative means. Ribbon/food gain value without a calculable utilitarian answer to “waste.” Nero's maxim about enjoying waste is quoted fiction; Monica tentatively tries it. | E009–012, E015, E021; C002 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-T-003 | Recipient asymmetry is produced through focalization and late disclosure: an act can be gratitude, threat, modesty or intrusion depending on incomplete access. Aaron's loss of memory and Cyril's broken continuity are formally rendered, not only explained afterward. | E001–007, E014–017, E020, E023; C007–008 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-T-004 | Recognition can invite agency or make worth dependent on another's approval. Monica, Cyril, Selma and Thornley occupy distinct versions; they are not morally or psychologically identical. Talent-as-curse is Monica's attributed view and motivates saving another's pride. | E003, E010, E014, E017, E019, E021–023; C010 |
| SW-CG2-20261019-T-005 | Fiction is an in-world resource for villainess cover, gestures, fantasy affiliation and ethical maxims. Agatha/Isabelle direct sound and costume; Nero/Lin test scripts with comic failures. Bridget's cool exterior/Cyril's “ice nobleman” reputation show a genre image misreading behavior. | E002, E004–007, E010, E015–016, E018, E020, E023 |

## Prospective questions and expectations

All opened only after V01; **no genuine prediction existed at entry**. No probability or outcome is invented. These are UNRESOLVED_QUESTION records, not claims about unread books.

| ID | Question, live alternatives and discriminating opportunity |
|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-001 | Does reciprocal ordinary recognition help Monica act without making her worth depend on perfect output? Compare subsequent chosen low-stakes contact, failed tasks and responses to acknowledgment; do not count compelled compliance alone as confidence. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-002 | Does Cyril identify his rescuer, and would that alter fairness, intimidation or recognition? His clues could remain unresolved, be denied or be confirmed; requires explicit knowledge/action evidence rather than the reader's identity certainty. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-003 | How would Felix's manipulation of Norton relate to reverence for Everett if he joined the identities? Neither immediate romance nor automatic remorse is predicted. Observe identification boundary, first appraisal and treatment under changed knowledge. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-004 | What does Felix's “remaining life” mean, and what does Will know? Literal danger, role transition or another undisclosed constraint remain possible. No conclusion without admitted disclosure; publication/genre familiarity is excluded. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-005 | Is the royal order fundamentally protection, monitoring or a combination? Louis's belief is plausible but not confirmation. Look for the king's account, independent operational goals or contradictions; retain factional bias. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-006 | What explains father-loss/talent linkage and rupture with Monica's former friend? Do later disclosures qualify her account? Do not fill names, causes or responsibility from the existing dream. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-007 | What are the consequences/limits of mental interference for victims and authorized practitioners? Compare recovery, institutional findings and later choices with Monica's uncertain justification. Silence about consequences is not proof of none. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-008 | Who is Louis's backup disciple, and how much effective protection did it supply? Current existence is reported privately, identity and interventions unknown. Nero's species also remains unresolved despite confirmed transformations. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-Q-009 | Can acquired council trust survive error, rank pressure and obligations independent of Monica? Track Lana's ambition, Bridget's dissent, Elliott's prejudice, Neil's work and Isabelle/Agatha's performance as autonomous evidence, not only protagonist support. |

### Institutions and magic — V02 changes

These extend the existing W responsibilities, retaining their V01 limits. W009's school distinctions remain; there is no new basis to alter the original historical school-calendar exposition.

| Record ID / continuity | Demonstration, attributed rule or bounded inference | Route and limit |
|---|---|---|
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-010; extends W001–002 | Capacity, formula understanding and manipulation are distinct. Instructor thresholds: 50 apprentice, 100 competent, 130 upper, 150 sage minimum, over 200 rare; Monica recalls 202, MacLagan demonstrates 160, Neil 96. Glen's >250 indication breaks the instrument and is initially treated as malfunction. Multiple simultaneous human spells and remote precision are difficult. Ritual courtesy cannot be elided like a construction formula. | V02:E002/E006/E027. Thresholds do not certify all-round rank or the exact cause of the broken meter. |
| SW-CG2-20261019-W-011; extends W004 | Ridill forbids human bodily manipulation, enhancement and healing because of risks including power poisoning. Schvargart's new emperor has permitted limited medical research; migration is reported/prospected. Louis describes authorized coercive mental interrogation as potentially devastating and offers to forgo it conditionally. | V02:E011/E028. Foreign policy exposition is storyworld presentation; no clinical label or external medical recommendation. V01 mental-spell legality/consequences remain unsettled. |

### Readiness, study boundaries and transaction

SW-CG2-20261019-RD-V02: **UNASSESSED** for every subject and domain of operational reconstruction. More ordinary variation and directional evidence are retained, but no commissioned rules, models, independent domain tests or readiness award exist. This is not a finding of source insufficiency. No global identity or capability record is created.

All future specialist dispositions are reviewed through V02: disclosure/focalization, magic/material expertise, rank/political protection and affiliation/deception remain MONITOR for a later assigned substantial argument; Japanese speech/comedy/ethics and loyalty/agency remain MERGE_INTO_EXISTING reading and cumulative work. Romance as a settled reciprocal thesis remains NOT_SUPPORTED. Dedicated character/dyad products and integrated synthesis remain DEFER under this one-unit assignment, despite substantial retained evidence. Cross-witness/history/adaptation comparison stays outside scope. The master and local routes supply their evidence, not a claim that those later products are complete.

Complete original V01 sections, IDs, freezes and source boundaries remain intact.

New C011–013 have V02 origins only. Q review distinguishes partial opportunities from UNTESTED identity/king/recovery questions. Independent semantic acceptance, source-selected reviewer sample and coordinator local persistence remain pending. No Git, publication, authority promotion, whole-corpus freeze or self-acceptance is performed. ## V03 cumulative state

This section adds SW-LNJP-V03 only to the complete nonzero V01–V02 work above. The [V03 reading](../01%20Sequential%20Readings/SW_CG2_V03_DEEP_READING.md) owns sustained argument, causal synopsis, PRE_V03 at **2026-10-20 02:08:29 UTC**, verbatim preceding exit-freeze lineage, E-V03-001–045, complete actual source/visual/paratext coverage and POST_V03. Short `V03:E001` means SW-CG2-20261019-E-V03-001. All V03 routes bind to EPUB SHA256 `38989102ddc9848024e67fd9d241bb4a1d4b24db166d39b663c7311a2d86efa9`, 16,512,601 bytes, and hash-matched original XHTML. Its `p-NNN:Pn` rule counts every original one-line paragraph from one within `OEBPS/Text/p-NNN.xhtml`, including blank/image/heading paragraphs. Earlier `V01:E...` and `V02:E...` retain the original witnesses/hashes specified above. This ledger provides recovery routes and bounded propositions, not condensed replacement reading.

### Character processes — V03 additions to actual subjects

The updates below accumulate rather than overwrite earlier processes. A recipient-specific act is not a global disposition, and a new represented memory is placed at its story time. Observation, private thought, character account and inference stay distinct in the reading.

|---|---|---|

The body still trembles and his selfishness judgment returns after the Mira rescue. Public chess concentration, chosen play and limited flight are genuine new actions; solitary crowd terror, touch fear and failed landings persist. He accepts Casey's anonymous delivery despite calling her foolish/emotionally susceptible. | E025/E041/E043–045; C006/C007/C013; L024 |

|---|---|
| **T012 Attachment as knowledge and exposure** | Particular relational history outperforms nearly identical numerical shape, then gives hostage leverage. Same attachment supports detection, ethical purpose and vulnerability (E027–031). Rival “feelings simply improve” fails at poisoning; rival “feelings merely disable” fails at detection/Ray rescue. |

Shared object does not ensure shared custom; desired self-belonging differs from claiming another (E007/E011/E026/E031–035/E039). Parallel is strong formal inference, not a solved future death/identity. No universal anti-ambition moral. |
| **T015 Help and fallible evaluation** | Myra's fear, Cyril's duty story, Louis's heartlessness model and Eliane's audience calculations each partly misread another; tailored help can work without total access. Recognition's value can be actual while its account is false (E011/E024–025/E029/E031/E035–037/E040). Not all mistaken theories have equal ethics or outcome. |

Q031 receives bounded help with partial disclosure; Q032 flower delivered; Q033 flute destroyed without testimony; Q036 Lin freed/Reruva dead/cores angrily released, separate Darwin/flute aims but no direct duke order. Q021/Q027 gain convergent substitution evidence/Bridget's feared death, not performed operation. Q034 costly refusal untested, Q035 setback/bounded learning not durable cure. Father trial/Grail, remaining life, old victims, maternal explanation, ghost relation, Barney repair, school future and quiet-containment consequences remain open.

New questions, first formulated after full V07, copied literally from POST_V07:

**Q037.** What can Monica and Yuan actually corroborate when they compare their evidence, and what does the unnamed master's announced journey change? Opportunity: a performed meeting, documentary/physical evidence, explicit refusal or incompatible accounts. Falsifier to a simple truth-delivery expectation: continued strategic withholding, wrong identification or an encounter serving a different aim. No meeting or disclosure predicted.

**Q038.** Can Bridget continue to seek truth after the hope of reunion is lost without treating Monica's partial cooperation as unlimited service? Opportunity: her response to new evidence, a respected refusal, a renewed threat or a changed aim. Her own admission of feared death is the origin; neither recovery nor vindictive destiny is assumed.

**Q039.** What persists of the boys' spirit encounter when Romaria can speak or act again and Mary's promised management meets an actual new conflict? Opportunity: renewed spirit choice, consent, communicated dissent, ongoing confinement, retaliation or fulfilled stewardship. The gift and cessation of attack do not guarantee reconciliation.

**Q040.** Will Mary's wish to save an unnamed person explain a specific future choice without making all imposed tests necessary? Opportunity: named recipient, acknowledged uncertainty, contrary consequence or changed means. Bartholomeus's male guess remains a guess; no identity forecast is registered.

**Q041.** Does the present Felix acknowledge any relation to the scarred servant's gifts and punishment, and how does that account relate to his present elective coercion? Opportunity: an explicit identity account, contradictory witness, reliable operation record or a costly choice about someone else's freedom. The convergent theory does not authorize treating every later act as compelled.

### New prospective questions - first registered after V08

**Q042.** How do the assembled recipients answer Monica's disclosed identity and request, and can she respect a refusal or condition while pursuing rescue? Origin E032 against E018/E030. Opportunity: actual addressed reaction, freely limited cooperation, rejection, renewed coercion or disagreement. Falsifier to automatic reconciliation: a recipient treats the deception as a continuing injury or declines help. No response predicted.

**Q043.** What exactly does the diary's final correction say, and can it reach Isaac in a form he can receive rather than convert into another memorial command? Origin E024/E027/E031. Opportunity: displayed last words, actual delivery, interrupted/refused reading or an incompatible interpretation. Neither a cure nor freedom by information alone is presumed.

**Q044.** Can the Black Grail be manufactured and tested within the remaining time, and what can its result actually establish before hostile decision-makers? Origin E028–029. Opportunity: supplies/equipment/labor obtained or refused, technical failure, control/counterexample, contested interpretation. The blueprint and Monica's confidence do not already constitute a functioning proof.

**Q045.** At the hearing, how do Isaac's intended villain performance, Clockford's convenient account, the preserved evidence and Monica's war constraint interact? Origin E026–027/E032–033. Opportunity: actual testimony, procedural exclusion, changed factional incentives, refused inference or invoked imperial bargain. No execution, exoneration, succession or prevented war is forecast.

**Q046.** Can Will's wish to help Isaac find a nonfatal form that respects the distinct wishes and consent of those involved? Origin E031 against E014–015/E027. Opportunity: requested cooperation, refused sacrifice, a changed contract, communicated new wish or repeated self-erasure. His devotion and Monica's refusal alone do not settle future agency.

### Institutions, bodies and magic constraints

| New route | Constraint and what it excludes |
|---|---|
| **W062 Blood analysis** | Device built by coordinated infrastructure and narrowed function, controls/power conditions/nonmagical washing matter. Real device does not authenticate sample identity; hidden bottle changes inference. E005/E012/E017. |
| **W063 Combat / coalition** | Two-spell limit, mirrors and barrier-assisted blows impose specific limits. Monica's remote support ends before Cyril's last stand; Louis's contrived trip scrapes Cyril's palms, then his grip interrupts Cyril's chant after considered brooch removal. Induced poisoning drives the incomplete freeze; it is dangerous self-risk, not controlled mastery or rescue of a present Monica. Nonmagical curse plus flowers buys time; no general superior strength or safe overload. Later reprisals continue. E007–009/E014. |
| **W064 Hearing authority** | Permission to speak, petitions of bounded reach, staging, physician recommendation and fabricated curse produce a medically managed royal withdrawal. Actor assertions distinguished from objective history. E010–013. |

| **W067 Empire / sages / exchange** | Immediate war averted under interested bargain. Medical/magic testing/trade ambitions prospective; Raul has arranged next-year study abroad while research continues remotely. Vacant sage seat not filled, reduction to five speculative. Selection weighs experience/research, recommenders and politics; Louis recognizes Glen's large remaining potential but says now is the time to develop it, not appoint him. Raul's friend wish and attributed approval, Ray's hoped romantic colleague and Raul's re-sorting preserve private motives beside institutional work. No completed mastery/readiness or abuse acquittal follows. E020/E022/E025/E029; L112/C006 corrective review/C022 local. |
| **W068 Labor / market / ordinary life** | Funding, design, workshop fit and logistics enable craft; commercial and affectionate motives coexist. ### Formal and thematic propositions

| New route | Proposition, rival and diagnostic evidence |
|---|---|
| **T043 Anger / recognition** | Anger need not terminate friendship; reverence can estrange it. Restored ordinary practice under joined identity is more specific than generic acceptance or cure. E001–003/E021/E029. |
| **T044 Collective competence** | Independent aims, bounded skills and resource access can defeat a planner's expectation without overpowering him or equalizing cost. Full voluntary harmony is checked by Monica/Hubert's coercive sampling of Barney, bait and reprisals. E003–009/E014. |
| **T045 Fidelity / correction** | Read-to-the-end delivery changes memorial obligation because Ark imagines future reunion and remembered rather than public immortality. Strong local effect does not reduce all coercion to missing information. E017–020/E028. |
| **T046 Proof / provenance / settlement** | Authentic operations can yield a false public inference; political effectiveness and justice remain separate evaluations. Neither all-machine-fraud nor all-truth-useless fits controls, substituted blood and incomplete vindication. E005/E012–016. |
| **T047 Staged authority / focalization** | Costume/script/permission make a public voice while viewpoint returns pain and fear to its body. Later disclosed preparation is not later-created agency or earlier reader knowledge. E006/E010–011/E016–018. |
| **T048 Chosen ordinary future** | Return to the world is independently chosen after the forced original departure. Lana-directed move, shared discoveries and coffee support desired ties beyond utility, while neglected needs and possessive methods remain. E023–024/E027–029. |
| **T049 Closure / distributed futures** | Supporting careers, montage and reference retrospection extend independent lives; performed concert differs from unfinished suite. Final dedication's noisy witch supplies formal closure, afterword corroborates design without curing characters or licensing future knowledge. E025–030. |

| **Q046** | Will freely continues specific contract and helps administration; fatal illusion not performed. His choice does not establish consent for others' relations/residence. E020/E028–029. |

### Genuinely prospective questions — first registered after V09

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-047.** How will Monica exercise teaching authority with a pupil whose technical responsiveness and domestic care coexist with jealous access-control and meanings she has not understood? Origin E028–029/R075, not an earlier prediction. Opportunity: actual teaching, communicated residence terms, requested/refused help, correction accepted or evaded. Falsifier to automatic rank-reversal equality: another bargain is accepted without comprehension or a refusal is overridden. No abuse-free household or reciprocal romance forecast.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-048.** Can Venedict's working research gain accurate credit or legal consequence without the substituted sample's success being mistaken for faithful proof? Origin E012–013/E018/E030 and C026. Opportunity: independent testing, explicit provenance disclosure, archival/trial evidence, public credit or refused vindication. Rival outcomes include continued concealment or technical recognition without legal correction. No future exoneration predicted.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-049.** What happens when the proposed imperial exchange and Raul's arranged study meet actual institutional obligations, continuing research and national competition? Origin E020/E029/W067. Opportunity: enacted travel, contracts, verified research, funding/resource disagreement or refused secrecy. Falsifier to post-hearing universal peace/harmony: a concrete conflict of national or personal aims. An arranged future study is not credited as attended.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-050.** Can Monica and Lana maintain wanted ordinary proximity alongside commercial use, limited time and Monica's neglected daily needs? Origin E024/E027–029/R004/R070. Opportunity: nonuseful shared time, accepted/refused work, design/credit disagreement, new care arrangement or a chosen boundary. Strongest rivals are pure exploitation and affection making every demand harmless; neither is predicted. The move itself is already accomplished, not a prospective success invented after the fact.

### Institutions, mathematics, magic and material life

| New route | Demonstration/report, consequence and limit |
|---|---|
| **W069 — medical science / regulatory route** | Anonymous ally has medical-association/guild influence, helped preserve designs and reports already speaking to both bodies. Plans for device deregulation may reopen forbidden-research adjudication; “next year” in the winter scene is not enacted law or full Venedict exoneration. E002; Q048/Q051. |
| **W070 — contractor death / spirit gem** | Mirror water spirit could choose freedom or sleep in a remaining gem; sleep depletes power and cannot replenish by an absent new contract. Finding and supplying mana resolve immediate appeal, not final legal custody. Do not universalize one choice to all spirits. E011/R077. |
| **W071 — possession / habitat** | Rare matching affinity/reserves/body conditions permit possession; altered pigment/overlapping signatures and possible lethal poisoning distinguish it from a spirit merely adopting animal shape. Decreasing high-mana habitat displaces spirits. Puppy/weak wind spirit case requires care for both. E012/R078. |
| **W072 — spirit offerings / human music** | Wind spirits value music but have particular tastes; Lin's song functions like a chant and could generate an attic storm. Good hymns differ from a desired remembered work song. Spirits need no human food/organs; Lin's remembered feeling cannot be reduced to measurable human pulse. E012/E017. |
| **W073 — agricultural contamination** | Enchanted plant resilience competes with edible-part mana poisoning and soil leakage; severe land pollution draws magical beings and excludes habitation, requiring isolated fields. Some beans absorb soil mana. Proposed field layout still depends on slope, drainage and tests. E024; Q053. |
| **W074 — school / labor / material privilege** | Treasurer's cash checks constrain corrupt teacher; teacher authority/extra work bypass scrutiny. Noble music/royal-history curricula, counselor remedies, room records, club nonmembership fairness and servants' hidden baking work structure choices. Clothes paid through covert mission and teacher holiday rescued through a student's sleep are unequal distributions, not neutral “support.” E010/E012–013/E016/E018/E021/E024. |
| **W075 — ordinary skills and bodies** | Butter softening differs from melting; handwriting/drawing/icing do not share one motor medium; numerical order may be unusable to others. Cold tolerance does not follow automatically from ice affinity; Nero's heat tolerance is draconic despite cat form. Household care, supplies, fitted clothes and curtains require labor/resources beyond magical capacity. E001/E005/E010/E018/E025–026. |
| **W076 — Mira / auditory work** | Sentient device's dangerous fixation helps determine female custody; Louis's release requires minute power control and listening for barrier fracture. Talking about family distracts its speech for this procedure, not a universal power of love or safe change in its disposition. E016. |


### Formal and thematic propositions

| New route | Proposition, counterpressure and route |
|---|---|
| **T050 — receiving before self-reproach** | Kindness can change its recipient's appraisal sequence: new joy/thanks at the ribbon contrasts explicit earlier confusion/self-blame. Continued fear, indecision and directive care limit a total-cure reading. E001/E003/E025; C003/RV061. |
| **T051 — particular recipient / revised form** | A remembered song, simpler accompaniment, moderated gift, light errand and a chosen name work through different histories; finer technique or larger expenditure is not automatically a better response. Refused singing/transfer, Casey's burden and Lin's mistaken experiment preserve limits. E007–014/E017–018/E021/E024–026. |
| **T052 — expertise / translation / medium** | Mathematical pleasure has many functions but does not understand every assignment, another person's motive or another motor medium. Specific nonnumerical appreciation and acknowledged icing failure are counterexamples to universal mathematical affect/general genius. Benjamin and Raul have parallel selective expertise. E009–012/E018/E024/E026. |
| **T053 — retrospective disclosure / actual change** | New old scenes deepen prior knowledge without making every remembered disposition a present regression. Desire for solitude was once relief; present chosen proximity retains solitary pleasure. Original predictions and prior reader ignorance remain dated. E001–006/E015/E023–026. |
| **T054 — recognition without shared category** | A comb's beauty, an apron cookie, a bouquet and scar-conscious dress may convey care without full disclosure or common romantic meaning. Actual private delight is evidence; it does not authorize reading every observer's speculation as avowal. E009/E014/E019/E022/E024–026. |
| **T055 — hidden institutional labor** | Unseen checks, report selection, teacher respite, kitchen help and covert mission funds shape what looks like individual success. Useful effect and legitimate responsibility do not guarantee fair costs or informed beneficiaries. E010/E013/E016/E018/E021/E024. |
| **T056 — comic correction / incomplete closure** | Threatening knife/bacon, ominous beauty/sleep, soul-reading/metaphor and future mirror/caller make first impressions corrigible. Actual injury and some unanswered questions resist making all danger harmless. Visual treasures and letter/cookie frames collect a life without closing every relation. E011–012/E017/E020/E023/E026–028. |

### New genuinely prospective questions — after EXTRA09 only

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-051.** Will the anonymous medical ally's planned deregulation become enacted change, and what would it actually do to the forbidden-research charges and Venedict's public credit? Origin E002/R076/W069. Opportunity: an enacted rule, named hearing/archive evidence, accurate credit, blocked reform or continued concealment. The prologue's “next year” is relative to its winter, not an analyst's forecast for the next assigned volume. Neither successful Grail work nor the ally's influence guarantees exoneration.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-052.** What becomes of the mirror water spirit's requested release/management and of the wind spirit actually transferred with the puppy, and whose preferences govern either disposition? Origin E011–012/R077–079/K092–093. Opportunity: actual custody/treatment, expressed choice, separation of animal/spirit needs, refused contract or further depletion. These are distinct named-by-route cases, not an answer about Romaria. Gratitude or one accepted transfer would not falsify every concern about institutional custody.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-053.** Does the proposed use of bean fields actually limit magical soil contamination without unsafe edible accumulation or displaced costs to neighboring land and workers? Origin E024/W073/K096. Opportunity: field trials, harvest measurements, failures, changed layout, eater/neighbor response or a reporting disagreement among Monica, Raul, Cyril and sponsor. Proposal, calculations and friendship alone are not a safe result.

### Institutions, magic and material conditions

| New route | Condition, use and remaining uncertainty |
|---|---|
| **W077 — penal/medical reform** | Medical-device deregulation follows useful Grail work; forbidden-use/research death penalty → life imprisonment is virtually settled, meeting final confirmation. Eventual deregulation of medical bodily research is an expected further step. Woodman named among advocates; improved Ray restraints materially reduce prison risk. Monica feels father's contribution and irrecoverable loss. Her Darrel sentence/classification assessment is prospective, not court ruling; Venedict vindication/sample disclosure absent. E001/E018; follows W069 without rewriting its earlier planned state. |
| **W078 — contracts / artificial hosts** | Normal contract requires rare costly processed stone and matching affinity/power; command not identical to absolute obedience though emergency restraint can be strong. Darrel cannot use his own body as contractor's stone substitute; another living body lets spirits survive/transfer power but constrains their full strength. Hans and the other artificial hosts differ from Rick's long growth; health risks need observation. Novelty does not give consent to appropriating a child. E012–016/E021. |
| **W079 — ecology / unequal spirit skills** | Mana-rich habitats diminish. Sea-born Ruferno has greater water strength than forest-spring Will, but does not share precise illusion skill. Water movement, independent swimming, severing frozen form, limited distant surface protection and contract communication each have different constraints. Human anatomy knowledge does not follow from water mastery. E010–012/E018/E024. |
| **W080 — summons / load / attention** | Ritual words cannot be omitted, although ordinary chant is replaced. New stabilization extends maintenance and permits concurrency, not costless indefinite gates. Flight needs limited height/load/precision, so ice repair, oars, illusion and flanking add distinct capacity; attacks must avoid allies/ship. E007/E016–018. |
| **W081 — governance / transport / labor** | Isaac must balance local officials, residents, oversight and fear of renewed succession ambition. Brendan can relieve Will and secure administrative aid by old connections. Flight reserves differ; boat towing is impossible over proposed distance. Artisan maintained wheels, outfit load, cold-transport magicware and actual delivery money shape gifts and travel. Reference costs do not equal performed studies. E005/E008/E018/E020/E023. |
| **W082 — roles / bodily care / information** | Employer silence may protect a stigmatized worker yet cannot make concealed danger harmless; institution can offer membership before restored trust. Badges/crest support chosen aspiration while burden remains. Map memory is insufficient for crowd navigation; a full rest day, food/bath/clothes and medical checks matter after victory. ### New genuinely prospective questions — first registered after V10

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-054.** How will Rick's body and choices develop after Ruferno's removal, and can his promised institutional place support repair without making health or worth depend on useful labor? Origin E021/K106/R090/R092. Opportunity: actual growth/medical findings, changed work/clothes, consulted/refused help, another breach or a respected choice. Visible strength and blue hair already persist; neither future illness nor safe recovery is predicted.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-055.** What would Brendan make of Isaac's identity if the prepared hidden-master account ceased to suffice, and how would remembered loyalty and accepted companionship interact? Origin E020/E024/K105/R086/R093. Opportunity: explicit disclosure, an independent recognition, changed service terms, refusal or continued concealment. Isaac's willingness to be killed is his forecast, not evidence Brendan would kill or automatically forgive.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-056.** Who attacked Neigal from outside, and what actually follows from Fallon's escape for the people Louis seeks to protect? Origin E025/K108. Opportunity: identified action/testimony, contrary culprit evidence, fulfilled or failed security, warning received or no attack. Louis's threat assessment does not make Fallon the bomber or a future assault certain; Darrel's chime recollection does not establish its mechanism.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-057.** Can a response to shrinking spirit habitat preserve a particular spirit's wishes without passing its survival cost to an unconsenting body or merely silencing the spirit? Origin E016/E019/E024/W078–079. Opportunity: affordable material provision, a requested/refused contract, actual release, new voiced will or a costly institutional alternative. Darrel/Ruferno's genuine affection is already established and remains compatible with wrongdoing; Romaria and the two EXTRA09 cases retain their own unresolved questions.

Raul mistakes Cyril's gift intention and promises him a wreath; Monica has not received the original. E006–007/E009/E015. |
| **R064/R085 continuation, Will ↔ Isaac / Monica** | Will warns Isaac silently before tea ingestion; later reports the preparation's potency/dose risk and questions its intended use. Isaac acknowledges detection before drinking and his inability to resist magical medicine, retains the bottle for stated research and refuses use on someone. Will agrees to tell Monica of excessive demands and receives her shared solidarity; Isaac acknowledges being a difficult master while continuing response-management. The relation adds a possible corrective route without a performed new refusal. E008/E020; C005/RV076, C028/RV075 at its unchanged support boundary. |
| **SW-CG2-20261019-R-096, Bradford ↔ Ugo** | Master reassures, assigns support, praises its performance and asks for attack improvement. Pupil trusts, communicates through a patron, shares sensed positions and admits a missed shot. Mutual confidence does not mean infallibility. E010/E015/E017. |
| **R097, Louis ↔ Owen** | Chosen trust gives former coerced accomplice a desired chance at repayment; Owen declines a different promotion, performs defense with the provided tool and brings a report. Gratitude does not erase the original threat or harm. E001/E010/E017/E021. |
| **R098, MacLagan ↔ Fallon** | Teacher recognizes the pupil's destructive wish and acts to stop it after regretted earlier inaction. Fallon uses an adapted fellow prisoner's formula and attacks through others; no remorse or received teacherly reconciliation. E013/E017. |
| **R099, Fallon ↔ Louis** | One seeks hostile recognition/equality while denying the other's standing; the other refuses rivalry because attacks target loved people. Fallon produces real danger, but cannot force the desired relational category. Louis's contempt is his own response, not a neutral account of every old fight. E002/E013/E016–017. |
| **R100, Raul ↔ Melissa / household** | Serum appropriation provokes deliberate substitution, but Raul's independent dosage error magnifies it. She conceals responsibility and desires Cyril; he does not yet know her part. Grandmothers control access to leisure, caretakers translate offered rest into work. E005–009. |
| **R101, Vanessa ↔ Hubard / Percy** | She values information, rights and useful social ties and imposes investigation; Hubard knowingly seeks pleasure/debt and accepts in exaggerated deference. Percy's signatures and armed intimidation are reported antecedents; his continuing network value preserves life conditionally. E022. |
| **R102, Isaac ↔ Baldia / unseen daughter** | Ally attempts a drugged sexual/dynastic encounter through adulterated tea. Isaac notices the rose warning, receives Will's alert and preserves the cup before removal can erase the evidence; no ingestion occurs. He permits removal, then answers with blackmail rather than prosecution; the guest-room scene establishes a confiscated bottle. Ordinary poison tolerance versus inability to resist magical medicine is Isaac's explicit report, not observed physiological resistance. Father's assertion of consent is not the daughter's voice. Will's later question checks a different possible victimization by Isaac. E008, p-007:P52–120/P155–168; corrected C005/RV076. |
| **R103, Monica ↔ Raul** | Correct quantities do not protect her from his mixing error; he rescues, offers bodily support and fears rejection while holding the grimoire. She actually accepts his hand, addressing her fear of Louis rather than consciously absolving Raul's lineage. E006–007/E011/E016. |
| **R104, Isaac ↔ Isabelle** | Shared scholarly admiration enables a jointly planned disguise and front-row pleasure; each has independent enthusiasm. Isaac also uses her relationship with Monica as leverage on correction. Cooperation is real, with Monica initially outside their plan. E019–020. |

### Knowledge and chronology

| New route | Established boundary and evidence class |
|---|---|
| **K109 — relative time without invented harmonization** | p-004:P71 says the prison explosion was about two months earlier; P274–275 schedules the lecture for mid winter-invitation month, the month after next. p-005:P4 dates the field visit to that month's second week/day three. p-013:P27 calls Darrel's ship encounter about two and a half months earlier. Retain those stated intervals without forcing an exact common calendar. Full-day recovery intervenes before university response; lecture is the next day. Secret episode is a summer retrospective. The winter recalled with Isaac was two years earlier (p-015:P270–272). E003–004/E006/E010/E016/E020/E022. |
| **K110 — fog knowledge distribution** | Reader first receives Fallon adapting Thornley's formula; Monica infers a Minerva misrecognition compound from names, dry fog and unaffected Cyril. Susceptibility depends on remembered Minerva, not sage rank; MacLagan avoids exposure, Bradford's conduct remains continuous. Duration hypothesis has specified countercase explanation. E011–015. |
| **K111 — recovered adult repertoire** | Louis is not physically transported in time. Mistaken student self lacks usable adult spell knowledge; seeing his own later Mirror Prison and taking the monocle returns identity and adult sealing. Monica's deliberately imperfect execution succeeds at that purpose. Apparent captured Fallon is subsequently disclosed as substituted illusion. E014, p-012:P63–113. |
| **K112 — five bell uses** | Forest/lake test; university compound; library seal destruction; Fallon's bodily amplification to use black flame; seven-shot remote attack. The fifth shatters the bell and later melting removes material evidence. No fivefold multiplier was stated. Reader sees the maker call it a self-costing replica; investigators have only fragmentary testimony and inference. E002/E013/E016–017/E021. |
| **K113 — actual dispositions** | Fallon captured, severe mana poisoning under treatment and unstable interviewing; family returns home; Louis rests with real stitched injury plus exaggerated report. Darrel reportedly cooperates quietly in questioning, not a supplied repentance or repaired child relation. All six university missing people accounted for; guard actually receives brooch. Grimoire recovered for sealing, but no final complete inspection report; plants/contamination/seals require cleanup. Proposed smoking is not a performed completed punishment. E015–018/E021. |
| **K114 — correct lecture / unintended rebuke** | Conrad infers crowd-management policy and hostile social meaning; private narrator explicitly denies the latter intent. Correct answer, admiration and gained courage remain real. Apparent guard is Isaac under proportion-changing illusion, not a novice magical convert. E019–020. |
| **K115 — identity, detection and assent remain local** | Before ingestion Isaac recalls the rose warning, receives Will's silent alert and identifies adulterated tea; P120 explicitly states detection before drinking and distinguishes ordinary poison tolerance from magical-medicine vulnerability. Will later reports potency/dose risk; neither statement is an observed drug trial. The sample is retained for Isaac's stated research, not a narrated experiment. Brendan knows apprenticeship, not Isaac's substitution; Will learns the former here. Isabelle knows both and coordinates disguise. Monica did not know Isaac's pre-servant family or the later disguise/trip plan in advance. Daughter's supposed agreement is Baldia's claim only. No complete residence/romance settlement. E003–004/E008/E020; p-007:P52–120/P155–188; C005/RV076. |
| **K116 — Rick / reference updates** | Rick's collision fear is false; avoidance skill and Ugo's injury are actual. p294 reference calls Rick twelve and reports Bartholomeus teaching literacy/arithmetic. This advances current reference age, not bodily recovery/normal-growth proof or a retroactive correction of V10's earlier eleven-approaching-twelve. Byron's marriage is narrated, not proof of the old handkerchief's original reception. E004/E010/E023. |
| **K117 — retrospective business / prospective winter** | Vanessa orders six months of Kerbeck-site investigation after a missed Grail opportunity; no built furnace, verified stability or current Monica encounter. Hubard anticipates a winter visit; Isaac's distinct business remains undisclosed. Bell-maker's old ice spirit, snow mountain and gate are future plans, not an identified/recruited ally or opened gate. Romaria's glow remains unvoiced. E015/E020–022. |

### Institutions and magic constraints

| New route | Mechanism and consequential limits |
|---|---|
| **W083 — actual field failure / reuse** | Out-of-season peas mostly fail; safer slow infusion differs from emergency liquid application. Ingredient substitution plus two-decimal misreading causes dangerous growth despite correct calculation. Reused mana absorption helps fog removal, but the pea is partly uncontrolled and must be restrained/disposed. No edible safety/harvest result. E005–007/E015/W073/Q053. |
| **W084 — controlled force / two-spell constraints** | Bradford's unreproduced sixfold formula balances speed/power and researched control. Sensing gives distance but not altitude; Ugo's sharing needs visual completion. Flight restricts shield form. Grimoire activation is device use, permitting two other spells. MacLagan's water reach/eyesight and Lin's device incompatibility demand unlike partners. E010/E016–017. |
| **W085 — barrier/land/networked stakes** | Large installed barriers usually draw high-mana land through tools. Fallon rewrites a university formula and links thirteen stakes; Monica repairs the original containment without exporting mental interference, Raul breaks fixing magic/pulls stakes. Residual mana persists after extraction. Authorized guild/patron routes affect response speed; rank itself does not confer spell immunity. E010–015. |
| **W086 — amplification and black flame** | Louis distinguishes amplification of land/formula/device from amplification of a human body, inferring different bodily costs; Fallon's severe poisoning is later reported. Five-use replica, not a measured universal multiplier. Black flame burns spell/barrier but has short range and finite propagation time; giant targets and separated gates exploit it. No material bell survives, no full maker/object theory. E016–017/E021. |
| **W087 — Roseburg compact / selective law / magical medicine** | Strong temporary and permanent preparations, restricted lineage secrets, Crown access and guaranteed family sage seat are a political bargain. Temporary infusion can be more strictly regulated because easier to abuse. In the seized-bottle scene Will reports strong aphrodisiac enchantment and dose-related mana-poisoning risk (p-007:P117/P121); Isaac expressly says ordinary poison tolerance cannot resist magical medicine and that he detected this tea before drinking (P120). These are attributed technical/capacity statements, not an observed successful resistance or a measured pharmacological result. Research/use/quasi-forbidden exceptions differ; Monica's supervisor role blocks exam help. These rules neither supply universal equality nor retrospectively acquit old mental intervention. E003/E008; C005/RV076, L147. |
| **W088 — furnace / resource extraction** | Metal enchanting without a magician needs enormous local mana; existing spirit/dragon habitat legally bars a furnace, unlike lower-draw tournament barriers. Later settlement, fire-spirit attraction and exclusion barriers remain costs. Just outside a danger designation is not verified safe/empty land. Vanessa seeks investment through a company; research order is not installed industry. E022/Q057. |
| **W089 — public research / institutional afterwork** | Original papers permit study without conferring mastery; stabilization lengthens formula and burdens Monica's mind without chanting. Cross-attribute testing/writing lag behind discovery. Publication changes textbooks, teachers' work and commercial editions. Restricted archives preserve dangerous knowledge for countermeasures but need intact seals. A battle victory does not certify rebuilt custody. E004/E010/E018–019. |

### Formal and thematic propositions

| New route | Proposition / counterpressure |
|---|---|
| **T064 — mnemonic misrecognition** | A modest imposed premise recruits the target's own memory to restrict a present self. Shifts from unnamed inner voice to names, material discrepancies and later focalizations make recognition causal. Cyril's lack of Minerva memory, MacLagan's protection and Bradford's continuity prevent one uniform regression theory. E010–016; C031. |
| **T065 — play / refuge / desired past** | A board game offers another life without actual repair; numerical retreat shelters bereavement; Fallon violently stages a wished-for victory. Shared category “fantasy” does not erase different consent, purposes and consequences. Memories can impede play or authorize help. E007/E009/E011–013. |
| **T066 — teaching / interpretation / restraint** | Correct instruction, knowing a pupil's desires, inspiring confidence and stopping harm are related but separable obligations. Monica chooses among imperfect models; learning a feeling exists differs from sharing it. Actual Will alliance tests the idea and reveals partial failure. E003/E010/E013/E018/E020. |
| **T067 — connections / adult vulnerability** | Louis's explicitly learned network protects what attachment exposes, while Vanessa's coercive extraction and Fallon's denied rivalry prevent a universal celebration of social ties. Louis's self-power boast sits in tension with actual help; developed cooperation is the supported distinction, not independence. E001–002/E017/E022. |
| **T068 — power's interfaces** | Correct numbers meet false ingredients, strong spirit cannot use a tool, impressive flame needs time, public paper resists replication. Technical constraints distribute agency and leave cleanup costs; they do not themselves settle permission. E005–007/E010–018/E022. |
| **T069 — public meaning / private manner** | Ugo's intentional heroic staging, Monica's unintentional intimidating economy, Isaac's disguise and Conrad's misread admiration are different presentation mechanisms. Narrator correction can preserve real competence/pleasure while rejecting an inferred motive. E004/E019–020. |
| **T070 — winter / resource / paratextual doubleness** | The maker's lost love of human activity coexists with current hostility; industry seeks exploitable mana within law and ecological risk. Afterword ruby visibly pairs formal commitment and colloquial voice but does not diagnose author/character identity. E021–023. |

### New prospective questions — first registered after V11

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-058.** Will Will use the agreed route to Monica when Isaac's wishes become excessive, and will either master accept correction that costs a wanted result? Origin E008/E020/R064/R085/C028-RV075. Opportunity: an actual complaint, refused demand, bypass, respected limit or changed protective plan. Falsifier to the strong reading of an effective corrective alliance: the route is silenced or merely used to persuade Will to comply. Agreement alone is not an exercised refusal.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-059.** Who is the replica-maker, what lies behind the song's vanished appeals and letters, and what follows from the intended snow-mountain recruitment and audience gate? Origin E002/E015/E021/K112/K117. Opportunity: named identification, contradicted memory, voiced old spirit's response, a failed recruitment or actual gate use. Romaria's glow is not enough to identify the intended spirit; the announced winter outcome remains intention, not prediction of success.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-060.** Can Raul's received friendship and friend-directed botanical work sustain limits, accountability and ordinary contact when family control, inherited fear or experimental costs intervene? Origin E005–009/E015–016/C030/R048/R100/R103. Opportunity: knowledge of the substitution, accepted/refused touch or visit, repaired damage, changed household permission or a friend disagreeing. The current accepted hand counters total rejection; it does not establish universal acceptance or safe agriculture.

**SW-CG2-20261019-Q-061.** What will the Kerbeck investigation and planned visit actually disclose about the site's mana, residents and possible furnace, and whose interests will govern any change? Origin E020/E022/W088/K117/R101. Opportunity: site findings, spirit/dragon/neighbor speech, investment accepted/refused, changed legal designation or Isaac's business disclosed. No factory, permit violation, Hubard attack or shared Isaac/Vanessa purpose is presumed.

Original V11 producer endpoints were **C031/RV075/R104/K117/W089/T070/L159/Q061**. **2026-10-20 V11-REPAIR01:** operative endpoint is now **C031/RV076/R104/K117/W089/T070/L159/Q061**, with only corrective C005/RV076 added; the original five V11 events are preserved. Dedicated studies and integrated all-work synthesis remain separately assignable and unbegun. Final personal readback and repaired-producer/source-recheck state are recorded in [run state](../00_RUN_STATE.md#v11-repair01-status); no independent acceptance or coordinator persistence is claimed here.

### Typed character processes and independent aims

| Stable subject | V12 process, counterexample and limit | Evidence / cumulative route |
|---|---|---|
| **S-MONICA** | Enjoys children's restrained gratitude and chosen small performance while monumental praise overwhelms. Learns unfamiliar craft, tests formulas and adapts to failure. Explicitly requests release, escapes restraint and later accepts a newly offered hand. Misreads romantic pulse as illness but responds differently to Cyril's prospective marriage and accidental exposure. Teaching shifts from copied schedule to learner-specific practical work and chosen first spell. Protects Norman through recommendation/requested preparation, without making Hubard safe; protects Ray through technical containment, self-blame analogy, less destructive sedation and planned aftercare. V12 indexes **SW-CG2-20261019-S-NORMAN** (ノーマン), **S-FRIDA** (フリーダ・ブランケ), **S-HENRIK** (ヘンリック・ブランケ), **S-THEODOR** (テオドール・ブランケ), **S-SIMON** (サイモン), **S-KAYAN** (炎霊カヤン), **S-ICE-KAYAN-OPPONENT-V12** (unnamed ice woman in summer interlude), **S-AURELIA** (オーレリア・ハーヴェイ) and **S-CURTIS** (カーティス・アシュリー). Existing subjects, including Adeline and Rutherford where already named, keep their keys and earlier boundaries. The provisional ice key preserves a strong possible maker continuity without merging identities or supplying a missing name; it is not the maker's identified prospective mountain interlocutor or Romaria. Local names Greg/Jeffrey/Laila/Gordon remain searchable at L161 without a speculative completed biography.

### Knowledge, chronology and actual dispositions

| New route | Boundary / evidence class |
|---|---|
| **K118 — chronology** | Main trip precedes New Year gathering/party. Kayan interlude is early summer **before prison explosion**; secret episode is early Winter-Calling month **before Monica's year-end return**. Engagement was arranged about half a year before its revelation to Ray. War was fifty years earlier despite Theodor's ten-year recollection. Prologue's future festival tradition is narrator-authorized prolepsis, not reading beyond V12. E001/E015–019/E025. |
| **K119 — unequal information** | Isaac/Azur share identity-sensitive political gratitude and the attributed security lead/proposed response at K125. Isaac sincerely does not recognize Samuel; no transmission to Monica is shown. Public royal recuperation story remains cover. Norman does not learn Isaac's identity/overheard conversation. Visitors do not know Hubard's ancient-device research. Monica initially lacks security deployment/Will-absence knowledge and later disguises the source of Will's report. Lana does not know human guest is Nero. Reader's Kayan killing knowledge is not official survey knowledge. E002/E004/E008–015/E025. |
| **K120 — village cause and mitigation** | Will's report says spirits gathered to mourn Kayan, raising mana unintentionally; he says they have already dispersed and expects mana to decline (p-009:P253–264). Death cause reaches the reader only through interlude. Conductive plates plus removed insulation divert mana temporarily. Client trust and immediate income do not recover just because mana changes. Norman's relief “village saved” is his appraisal; referral/arrangements are shown, all sales and future solvency are not. E009–010/E013/E015. |
| **K121 — rings / hidden research** | Ring seal blocks Hubard's magic until deciphered; letter asks teaching and separately recommends Norman. No enforced teach-until-release condition. Physical abuse persists. Hubard privately infers old furnace/device manufacture and missing materials; material's identity is omitted, neither observer nor analyst can fill it. E011. |
| **K122 — family story correction** | Ray's recalled prohibition and reported maternal enslavement support his belief, then Mary's scene receives an explicit narrator correction: actual love was misread through the mother's self-curse; attempted removal caused the deaths. Ray is not told. Adeline's first love/Frida's descent is privately disclosed, not a public confession or known match motive for the young couple. E021/E023. |
| **K123 — forecast / unnamed feeling** | Mary's overlapping dangers remain indistinct, no weakened-defense report is supplied. The king's actual new-sage/solidarity request is retained separately at K126. Specific half-world prediction is not given an exact referent/mechanism/time. Isaac thinks of Monica and interprets final embarrassment through loss; no actual eye loss or confirmed exclusive fulfillment. Monica's grief/Cyril's self-deception strongly imply unrecognized attachment, but no mutual romantic declaration. E019/E024. |
| **K124 — outcomes** | Glen has passed after friends' help, not by cheating or witnessed universal mastery. Hubard is sealed, not reformed; Norman recommended, not shown enrolled. Young sages lose practice bout; later curse crisis resolves under protective cover, with clear tears/embrace, not cured susceptibility. Frida remains engaged and open to liking; Aurelia match is declined by Cyril's requested arrangement, no final later choice. Kara matter remains withheld, Henrik apology actual, alliance only considered. E011/E014/E017/E019–024. |

### Institutions and magic constraints

| New route | Mechanism / limit |
|---|---|
| **W090 — dueling tools / environmental solution** | Prepared fixed devices draw ground mana but cannot track freely. Short-range knife disrupts manipulation, not all installed magic. Injury prevention still permits pain. Temporary shell infusion differs from a permanent device. Conductive plates/insulating rock and an identified route enable redistribution; not universal earth mastery or inexhaustible sink. E003/E008–010. |
| **W091 — spirits / ecology / bodily cost** | Concentrated mourning increases local mana without intending damage. Water spirit's sensing is medium-dependent and full form includes clothing. Ancient human shelter survives changed size/etymology. Cannibal taboo grants power and bodily regrowth while reducing lifespan; another spirit's intended one winter is not completed gate or world restoration. E006/E009/E015. |
| **W092 — development / labor / time** | Huge furnace demand/dragon attraction and legal habitat limits persist. Northern risk differs by species and region; designation is not safety. Transport, monopoly ties, worker safety, profit allocation by investment ratio/labor contribution, safeguards against later investment manipulation, and interim income constrain development. Referral and agreed terms do not equal installed works or universal ethical trade. E003/E010–012. |
| **W093 — curse mechanism** | Unlike formulaic magic, some curses arise through strong emotion against conscious intention. Ray's voiced complaint has a partial venting function. Overflow can turn tears, plants and objects into monsters distinct from Raul's controlled plants. Cutting reproduces the threat; his fear of harming a loved recipient accelerates it. Physiological danger is real even though the parental explanation is false. E021–022. |
| **W094 — containment / unequal force** | Two-year reconstructed anti-curse barrier works but lacks economical formula and cannot indefinitely hold Ray. Louis's duel barrier incorporates anti-curse technique and relies on skill/device quality. Raul's mana protects his plants from Ray; uninfused tools remain vulnerable. Sedating/paralyzing rose plus surprise de-escalates, not verbal cure alone. Earlier flat shields fail against side blast; later blocked sight conceals flight. E017/E022. |
| **W095 — marriage / rank / truth** | Royal recuperation cover limits public schedules; retired prince must route an imagined alliance via third prince. Noble marriage can legitimate adopted/common birth and sought magical lineage; Highorn rejects compelling spouse choice. Curse-family wealth and good pay coexist with stigma and coercion. Ray's formal headship differs from grandmother's financial authority. E002/E016/E018–020/E024. |
| **W096 — instruction / elemental practice** | Minerva's ordinary six-month theory-first rule mismatches an advanced independent theorist lacking actual casting practice. Seventh-section mana transfer affects sixteenth-section deployment; pupil finds cause after teacher names failure. Water/ice/earth emission is heavy and costly, while fast elements fit combat differently. A chosen first original spell is a program, not a completed invention. E013. |

### Formal and thematic propositions

| New route | Proposition and strongest limit |
|---|---|
| **T071 — performance has recipients** | Snow monuments, bath spectators, imposed villainess, controlled battle and petals produce different public meanings from private experiences. A persuasive picture can protect while concealing pain or uncertainty. Prose/illustration can expose different access rather than one proving consent. E001–005/E019–024/E026. |
| **T072 — restraint / fit / repair** | Knowing fear differs from respecting release; remorse/promise/accepted later hand are real but painful pinch prevents universal safety. Changing a lesson can acknowledge teacher error; a magic seal cannot itself make a teacher stop physical abuse. Ethical adequacy needs the recipient and consequential limit, not only correct technique. E005/E011/E013/E020–022. |
| **T073 — material memory / grief** | Shell custom, egg's white decorative line, archaic robe and private poem retain possibilities not identical to their origin. Lost meaning can be rediscovered/adapted without restoring a whole vanished age or claiming another's will. Kayan and ice spirit share grief but choose different relations to present human life. E003/E007–008/E015/E018/E023/E025. |
| **T074 — time is part of care** | Long-term factory does not provide tomorrow's money; a curriculum can lag one learner; a marriage arrangement precedes meeting/love; ancestral war remains economically active. An offered future is material hope without a completed outcome. E010/E013/E016/E019/E023. |
| **T075 — self-curse / reader correction** | Ray's report initially explains fear, then the narrator separates the actual parental history from his inherited explanation. Literal curses and the ordinary “I cannot be loved” analogy interact without becoming identical diagnoses. Reader knowledge cannot silently cure a character. E021–022. |
| **T076 — honesty / protective fiction** | Frida's blunt refusal to pretend love makes a possible relation more specific but hurts in timing. Cyril's polite maturity excuse protects public reputation while concealing his desire. Louis's cover story physically protects spectators without informed uptake. Truth's ethical value depends on address and costs, not a single confession rule. E019/E022–024. |
| **T077 — borrowed role / imperfect agency** | Lana's dress, Isabelle's villainess formula, Raul's thorns and Louis's battle convention become usable supports through adaptation. Monica need not internally become an elegant villainess to rescue; Cyril need not discover a flawless self before covering her. Borrowing can enable agency while imposed scripts can injure. E019–026. |

Barney actually paces/listens/helpfully walks with young Monica; this does not cancel later control or supply an apology. Felix's private political report and public presentation are different access routes; Elliott's resentment/obedience are not a new egalitarian conversion. E003/E007/E013. No identity disclosure to these early characters. |

Canonical subject IDs retain **SW-CG2-20261019-S-...**; no new alias splits an already indexed person. **S-GRAHAM-SANDERS** is a new retrieval key for the already named hero, not a newly introduced person; the other sages keep their existing keys. These rows add processes and limits, not trait scores.
