### Institutions, magic and material constraints

| New route | Mechanism / what it does not establish |
|---|---|
| **SW-CG2-20261019-W-103 — corps / work / distributed office** | Commander relies on deputy's administration, squad command, investigators, physicians and troops; duty transfer permits care. Moving from Minerva's confinement to corps apprenticeship gives a threatened child a real opening through leverage. Rank also permits coercion and double standards over medical access. E001/E004–006/E011/E013–014/E021/E025. No homogeneous institution or sole-genius rescue. |
| **W104 — duel economy / conditions** | Damage and casting consume reserves; bracelet at or below one-third eliminates, leaving time for protected recovery unlike lethal real-world first strike. Terrain/altitude and flight skill matter. Remote origin can work beyond inward reflection; economical formulae require calculation, and Monica's real-time skill changes the depletion model. Reserves slightly below Louis's do not mean lower efficiency. Physical exhaustion and later resource shortage persist. E018/E021–022. |
| **W105 — mental interference / access / penalty** | Research permitted while unauthorized use risks loss of license; Glen's unqualified status could lose future examination eligibility. Formula specificity governs release; incomplete control plus huge power broadens effect. Rosalie declines prosecution, Louis imposes supervised use; neither retrospective assent nor proof a formal penalty occurred. E015/E020/E024/E027. |
| **W106 — workshop / barrier failure / suitable force** | Aging unrepaired anti-spirit barrier, unsettled mana, attracted lower spirits, power interaction and structure failure produce distinct hazards. Major's updated low-leakage shelter resists flame/debris but not falling impact. Lin's strength could worsen runaway. Glass Castle supports temporarily, creates slope and allows selective safe collapse after evacuation/perimeter clearance. E012/E021; C036. |
| **W107 — medicine / physiological limits / body hierarchy** | Rosalie's qualification, mana-related training and medical priority are real; amnesia preserves acquired knowledge but causes suffering. Bodily-magic reform remains Woodman's project, not a completed new law in this prequel. Aging reserves decline individually after youthful growth; sage minimum 150 leads Birdland to retire at 152. Graham's retirement is bodily choice, not demonstrated threshold failure. E003/E009/E013/E016/E023. |
| **W108 — guild / sage selection / protection / public truth** | Factional obstruction and recommendation routes can exclude real merit; national retention/publicity and ceremonial ability shape protection. Publicly known gift or nomination does not disclose purpose. Protective covers for coerced Owen and for Glen differ from Nate's concealment of excessive retaliation; all alter accountability/access. E003–004/E011–012/E017/E020/E022–027/E030. No V12 law/commission outcome follows. |

### Formal and thematic propositions

| New route | Proposition / strongest counterpressure |
|---|---|
| **SW-CG2-20261019-T-082 — acquired manners / recipient fit** | Polished voice, eye aid and appearance are real practices that can reassure, misidentify, conceal violence or estrange. Sally recognizes shout; Rosalie wants remembered roughness; Monica fears trained gentleness. Neither one true delinquent beneath fake gentility nor cured gentleman fits the distribution. E001–003/E008–011/E013/E018/E024/E027. |
| **T083 — kinds of withheld knowledge** | Postal suppression, deliberate omission, inner/overheard meaning, false protective inference, legal cover and reader-only agreement are different mechanisms. Replayed utterance changes access rather than fact; Owen's viewpoint confirms an inference without authenticating all Louis's predictions. All-uncertainty and transparent-intention accounts both fail. E010–011/E014–015/E020/E022/E024–025/E029. |
| **T084 — protection as complementary work** | Flood, Glen's fire and factory each need several differently placed capacities. Barrier cannot erase every failure mode; timing, delivery, energy interaction, floor support, evacuation and clearance matter. Sole savior and helpless expert rivals fail; coordination does not itself certify consent/proportionality elsewhere. E001/E006/E021; C036. |
| **T085 — chosen burdens / recipient asymmetry** | Love can motivate office, preserve a beloved's other bonds and sustain public duty; it can also justify secrecy, jealous control, punishment and extracting a colleague's labor. Rosalie's stated desire prevents reducing all romance to ownership, but her pleasure does not authorize all other costs. E008–011/E019–030; C006/C034 revisions. |
| **T086 — ability / worth / appointment** | Contest is genuinely lost, excellence recognized and confidence relationally supported; vacancies, faction, national asset protection and chosen retirement still govern office. Louis's winner-rights doctrine is not universal narrator law. Winston rejects prize; Major transmits expertise without the office. E012/E017–018/E021/E023/E026. |
| **T087 — return / loss / reopened ending** | Jar once enabled departure; now marmalade marks an absent caregiver before a chosen family. Visit and ordinary talk accommodate missing home without resurrecting it. Supplement explicitly continues burdens and misfit after happy ending; afterword's free-agent alternative is hypothetical play. E028–032. |

| [School companions](../03%20Characters%20and%20Relationships/SW_CG2_SCHOOL_COMPANIONS.md) | Lana, Isabelle/Agatha, Casey, Barney, Elliott/Benjamin, Bridget, Neil/Claudia and ordinary institutional labor as independent histories; R003–004/R008/R012–017/R020/R025–027/R032–038/R043/R054/R070–073/R080/R082–084/R090. |

The products' closeouts give existing warrant categories and substantive bases. No quota dictated separate monographs, no absence of a monograph implies a thin source, and supporting subjects remain autonomous in the ensembles and existing readings. Affiliation/romantic ambiguity, loyalty/deception/self-authorship and disclosure are developed there rather than deferred as indices. Operational reconstruction/readiness remains uncommissioned and UNASSESSED.
